# Changelog

Newest first. The build stamp is `version.json` and the `?v=` on each script in `index.html`. Status of open work is `BACKLOG.md`. The queue is `NEXT_PLAN.md` §3. Agent skills are in `.cursor/skills/README.md`.

## 2026-11-03 14:23 ET: Build: the Glimmerwell fleet follows the Nov 3 lore rulings (D1431, D1440, D1447)
- What changed and why:
  - D1431 (red, SETTLED by Vincent, Tue Nov 3, 2026): Keeper-hold slips on the `?debug=1` fleet lot stay, but read as local and temporary, never as an order from the capital. In `glimmer_fleet.js` `LINES`: Glow Care's slip "CLOSED PENDING REVIEW. By order." -> "CLOSED PENDING LOCAL REVIEW."; the mast's "POWER CUT BY ORDER." -> "POWER CUT. LOCAL NOTICE."; Fleet Bay's "VEHICLES HELD BY ORDER." -> "VEHICLES HELD. LOCAL NOTICE." (all PLACEHOLDER until Story). The Keeper yard (the towed Cruiser) is unchanged.
  - D1440 (yellow, SETTLED): Bright Swap is its own lamp-swap stall, not the buy-back stall, with no Keeper cover. Smallest change: on a Keepers hold the stall keeps its normal art (`gw_swap_stall`) and the stall and Swap Clerk use their split lines (PLACEHOLDER). The cloth slip and the Clerk's "Covered up by order." line are gone; `gw_swap_stall_shut` art stays defined but unused. `L()` falls back to the split line when a hold has none. The spec drops "(the buy-back stall)".
  - D1447 (green, SETTLED): `accord` comes out of the fleet test's banned-word regex (the other words stay).
- Files touched: `glimmer_fleet.js`, `tests/glimmer_fleet.spec.js`, `tests/README.md`, `handoff/specs/GLIMMERWELL_VEHICLES_BUILDINGS.md`, `handoff/shots/glimmer_fleet_lore/` (new; Keepers-hold stall before and after at 390 and 320), `tools/GAMEJS_MAP.txt`, `CHANGELOG.md`, build stamp.
- Commit(s) + build id: claude/b9-fleet-lore (from claude/glimmer-fleet-lines-june, #282), build 20261103-1423.
- Tests run and result (widths, checks): `tests/glimmer_fleet.spec.js` at 320, 375 and 390: 30 passed, 0 failed (new test "D1431, D1440"). Zero console errors; save untouched, `v` 2.
- Decisions made (colour + reasoning): D1431, D1440, D1447 built as settled. Green (engineer): Bright Swap on a Keepers hold is "unchanged" rather than "closed for the night", the smallest change with no new line.
- Anything left unfinished: the three local slip lines and Bright Swap's Keepers-hold state are PLACEHOLDER for Story.

## 2026-11-03 10:11 ET: Glimmerwell fleet lines: June's pass (drafted by Claude in June's role, approved by the owner) (build 20261103-1918)
- What changed and why: D1346 left every line on the `?debug=1` fleet lot as PLACEHOLDER for June. June is out, so Claude drafted her pass in her role: all 31 player lines in `glimmer_fleet.js` `LINES` (4 signs; doors, the four civilians, the mast and the stall per hold; the Cruiser; the Waiting Local; the debug `exit` sign unchanged). New spec §10b "Lines for the placements (D1341)": text only for the crackdown (Cruiser getaway, Concierge fly-by), the pick at dawn (Concierge over Juniper's camp, the Glimmer-pick east road, the Keepers-pick fly-by) and Act 2 (the quarry mast and bay, Glow Care), with open questions (Q-G1 to Q-G7, unnumbered for the Chief of Staff). Spec PLACEHOLDER notes now read "June's pass (drafted by Claude in June's role, Vincent to approve)". A sub-agent no-new-facts review found no CHIP_SECRECY or wording breach; its 2 must-fix and 6 should-fix notes are applied. A second sub-agent then checked the copy against the design repo (STORY.md, CHIP_SECRECY.md, June's drafts in story/drafts/): four line fixes ("partner" is the in-game word for a Walkling, so the Glow Care sign drops it; the Mast Tech's "a year" becomes "since it went up"; Juniper's two §10b lines no longer repeat her own Beat 1 and P6g lines), Q-G1 to Q-G5 annotated with canon citations, and two new open questions (Q-G6: the word "relay" on a sign both paths see; Q-G7: Episode 1 `quarry_bought` vs the Act 2 placement). Q-G3 is then answered as Story's pick under D1341: Glow Care goes to Brightgate (§10's old "Cobblecrest or Brinewick" named Episode 1 towns), by default as the street front of CHIP_SECRECY's planned Tuning wing at Brightgate HQ (Iris to confirm, option A or B); Q-G7 is marked red for Vincent, since it amends D1341's wording. After main's B-12 (D1087: no mint for Glimmerwell), the §10b dawn line's "Mint light" becomes "A soft wine light", matching the Concierge's wine pool.
- Files touched: `glimmer_fleet.js` (`LINES` and its header comment only), `handoff/specs/GLIMMERWELL_VEHICLES_BUILDINGS.md`, `handoff/shots/glimmer_fleet_lines/`, `index.html`, `version.json`, `CHANGELOG.md`.
- Commit(s) + build id: claude/glimmer-fleet-lines-june, build 20261103-1918.
- Tests run and result (widths, checks): `tests/glimmer_fleet.spec.js` at 390 and 320: `PP_TEST RESULT: PASS exit=0 passed=9 failed=0 flaky=0` each, `RTDB_RULES RESULT: PASS`; `node tools/scans/run.js`: all PASS (wording, decisions, symlinks, gamejs_map, test_mistakes).
- Decisions made (colour + reasoning): the owner approved the copy (Tue Nov 3, 2026) and settled Q-G3 as option A (Glow Care is the Brightgate street front of the planned Tuning wing). Q-G7 (red) is deferred to before the Cobblecrest build. Q-G1, Q-G2, Q-G4, Q-G5 and Q-G6 stay open with their defaults in spec §10b.
- Anything left unfinished: Iris's sign-off on voice; the design spec POST_TRIAL_CRACKDOWN §10 ("Glimmerwell in this quest is only Juniper") needs Design to add the D1341 Cruiser and Concierge. Each D1341 placement is still its own build.

## 2026-11-03 17:05 ET: No more mint for Glimmerwell: text and art go wine (B-12; D1087, D1448)
- What changed and why:
  - D1087 (Vincent, Tue Nov 3, 2026, SETTLED): "no more mint color" for Glimmerwell, anywhere; wine replaces it. PR #294 already moved the vests. This sweeps what was left.
  - Player text: every remaining Glimmerwell "mint" is re-voiced to wine or to no colour, same meaning, 110 or less: lamp light and glow (wine-red), ink, stamps and stars, stickers, the tag, the bow, the slide, the envelope and the Associate blurb (wine); Rook's lanyard (Glimmerwell lanyard); the Glimmer stand on Ribbit (#GlimmerStand); the pens (Company pens); the RegenX tank and scanner lines name no colour (their art is RegenX's own and stays). Handles: `mintwatch` is `lanyardwatch`, `mintcondition` is `freshvest`.
  - Verbatim design data (`cobblecrest.json`, `act1_route2.json`) is not edited (AGENTS rule 8). The game copies have moved past their design sources, so a verbatim copy would revert shipped lines; instead the new `data/detail_zz_no_mint.json` re-voices the bunting, the two Route 2 openers and Rook's "mint sticker" with `lineSwaps`, and the bunting prop and the two Cobblecrest "cord" lines (D1448: lanyard) with `objPatch.linesSet`, the way #294 did Mrs. Calloway. Design branch `claude/b12-no-mint` (39a1e31) carries the same wording in the design sources.
  - Art: mint `l` becomes wine `V` (no new colour) in the Comfort Lamp glass and motes, the lantern stand's jars, the quarry bunting, and the fleet preview art (`glimmer_fleet.js`: cruiser lamps and underglow, Sky Concierge lights, relay mast lamp, Bright Swap jars, badge, banner, sign, bay floor lights and the searchlight pool).
  - Kept, with reason: Clerk Mint (a name; also the `mintdesk` handle and `cc_mint` ids), the herb rack and the mint sprout (plants), the `mintcup` handle (tea), ids and lore tags (`thing.mint_vests`, `hm_mint_vest`), the RegenX inlay, tank and scanner art (RegenX's own, GLIMMERWELL_PALETTE §12), plant highlights on Glimmerwell planters and ledges, and the cracked handset's green terminal (D1100 as revised by Vincent: it stays green).
- Files touched: `data/detail_zz_no_mint.json` (new), `data/glimmer_path.json`, `data/heights.json`, `data/rook_crossroads.json`, `data/detail_zz_brinewick.json`, `data/detail_signal.json`, `data/detail_hushwater.json`, `data/detail_lizzy.json`, `data/detail_zz_partner_day.json`, `data/news.json`, `data/ribbit_posts.json`, `data/town_board.json`, `data/town_board_copy.json`, `data/civic.json` (note only), `data/mapdata.js`, `ribbit.js`, `quests.js`, `standing.js`, `sprites.js`, `glimmer_fleet.js`, `tests/no_mint.spec.js` (new), `tests/glimmerwell_dark.spec.js`, `tests/news_glimmer_dark.spec.js`, `tests/rook_foreshadow.spec.js`, `tests/arbiter_test.spec.js`, `tests/rx_f4_pilot.spec.js`, `tests/seals_both_paths.spec.js`, `tests/README.md`, `handoff/shots/no_mint/` (before and after at 390 and 320), `CHANGELOG.md`, build stamp.

Design: `claude/b12-no-mint` 39a1e31 (cobblecrest_check 3 stale-preview problems before, pass after; act1_route2_check and interiors_check pass before and after).

With the 24 specs covering the touched files (news, news_glimmer_dark, ribbit, glimmer_route2/cobble/fernbrook/briefing, glimmer_fleet, glimmerwell_dark, rook_crossroads, rook_foreshadow, lore_copy_wine, arbiter_test, rx_f4_pilot, seals_both_paths, brinewick, hushwater, lizzy, partner_day, town_board, standing, civic, heights, cc_cottage_roof): 314 passed, 4 skipped, 0 failed. ## 2026-11-03 16:52 ET: Build: town control engine and the Act 3 switch hooks (ACT1_KEEPER_JOIN K1, K2; D1504-D1506, D1513, D1514, D1536, D1558, D1561)
- What changed and why:
  - K1 (D1504, D1505, D1506): the map `hold:` reader is now `holdCond(F, c)` in `game.js`: `|`, `&`, `!`, `=` and `>=`/`<=`/`>`/`<` on a plain flags object, read the way `cond()` reads them (an unset number counts as 0). Fernbrook is marked contested (`sway: true`) and gains three rules ahead of D1171's built flip: with `story.keeper_cadet` and `story.side_pick_done`, `story.sway_fernbrook >= 2` Keepers, `<= -2` Glimmerwell, else split with a new line ("Keepers walk the lanes. Glimmerwell keeps the gate.", PLACEHOLDER).
  - Per-town sway `story.sway_<node>` is a whole number kept in -3..3 (`SWAY`, `clampSway`, `swayOf`); the `inc` step clamps it. Additive `S.flags` values, save v2; nothing writes one at load.
  - `fbPlazaPhase` reads `mapHold('fernbrook')` on the same flags `flagOf` reads (live or saved) once the Cadet read is on: `held_keepers`, `held_glimmer`, or `after` for split. New `fbplaza:post` (D1561, Vincent, Nov 3 night): a Cadet's Glimmerwell-held Fernbrook keeps the plaza Constable at his post (his tile, `hold`, no patrol); his Walkling, the other Keepers and the banner still go. The explaining line is later copy.
  - Inert until `story.keeper_cadet` exists: nothing writes it yet, so every save today reads Fernbrook, the place chip, the map line and the plaza exactly as before (the inert rows of the new spec pass unchanged on main).
  - K2 (D1513, D1536, D1558): `standing.js` `openAct: 3` (was 5, D161), `oneWay: 'glimmer'`; `canSwitch` says `one_way` for any join that is not Glimmerwell (with `to` null, the other side of `from`). Rook's offer stays the only switch; for `one_way` he says the same wait line as for `locked`. Offer copy for Act 3 is the Act 3 packet's.
  - E6 (D1506, D1559): `switchSide` to Glimmerwell moves every `story.sway_*` the save holds by `swayShift` (-2), clamped. Reports stop on `story.switches >= 1`.
  - D1514: `story.warden_cut` reserved; `PPStanding.reportsOpen(flags)` and `PPStanding.loyalistOpen(flags, act)`; read-only hooks `PixelPets.reportsOpen`, `PixelPets.loyalistOpen`, `PixelPets.sway(node)`, `PixelPets.holdLine(id, flags)`.
  - Dev checks: the map check covers the sway matrix, its edges, that the built flip ignores sway without a Cadet, and `holdCond` ops; the standing check covers `oneWay`, `swayShift`, `openAct`.
- Files touched: `game.js`, `standing.js`, `quests.js` (`NOT_STEPS` gains `story.sway_fernbrook`, `story.warden_cut`), `data/detail_zzz_fernbrook_plaza.json` + `data/mapdata.js` (the Constable's `when`), `tools/GAMEJS_MAP.txt`, `tests/act1_town_switch.spec.js` (new), `tests/standing.spec.js`, `tests/act_number.spec.js` (comment), `tests/README.md`, `README.md`, `CHANGELOG.md`, build stamp.
- Commit(s) + build id: claude/act1-k1-k2, build 20261103-1652 (restamped 20261103-1656 after merging main with #294). Built from design `master` (290033f, ACT1_KEEPER_JOIN v0.1.1 plus D1561).
- Tests run and result (widths, checks): `tests/act1_town_switch.spec.js`, `tests/standing.spec.js`, `tests/fernbrook_plaza.spec.js` at 320, 375 and 390: 93 passed. `tests/act_number.spec.js`, `tests/rook_crossroads.spec.js`, `tests/side_badge.spec.js`, `tests/data_integrity.spec.js`, `tests/map_legend.spec.js`, `tests/faction_or_gate.spec.js` at 320 and 390: 124 passed. The five inert tests also pass unchanged on main. Zero console errors; save v 2. Scans: wording, decisions, gamejs_map, test_mistakes PASS.
- Decisions made (colour + reasoning): D1504-D1506, D1513, D1514, D1536, D1558 (red, settled) and D1561 (yellow, settled) built as written. Green (engineer): the sway shift applies only on a switch to the one-way side (a debug free switch back moves nothing); `one_way` answers with Rook's existing wait line, so play reads as before.
- Anything left unfinished: K3-K9 write `story.keeper_cadet`, the sway steps, the Act 3 offer scene, the loyalist choice and every new line. `actOf` gives Act 3 for 5 Seals only once `story.ep1_end` is set (#297's rule, unchanged).

## 2026-11-03 16:46 ET: Build: the Weir Hut at Hushwater's Old Weir (WEIR_HUT v0.1.1, D1590-D1599)
- What changed and why:
  - Vincent approved the weir hut previews (rule 9: yes) and took every default (D1590-D1599, Tue Nov 3, 2026). Built from design master `specs/WEIR_HUT.md` v0.1.1 (f4fdb91).
  - `data/detail_zz_weir_hut.json`: the packet's overlay, copied verbatim (D1599). It adds the room `hw_weir_hut_in` (10x12, "Weir Hut", PLACEHOLDER name, D1592) and, on `hw_cut`, the 4x2 hut (turf roof, rock wall, barn door, slit window), the five-cell towpath, the oil lamp, rain barrel and nets, a trimmed fifth wild zone and a new first line on `hw_weir_s` (D1590, D1591, D1597). Its `objPatch` opens `glen_east`, `hw_falls_west`, `hw_falls_east` and `hw_cut_west` at `story.cobble_end | story.warden_met` (the log goes at either), and Ottilie and Nils wait for `story.cobble_end`; the Rim and the quarry stay shut (D1594). The door is barred with its deny line until `story.warden_met | story.cobble_end` (D1595).
  - `sprites.js`: skin `weir_hut` in `HOUSE_SKINS` (wall row `ICFKmFFFFcsB`, flags with two variants, sacking rug and mat, the weir trough and planks) and `WEIR_HUT_ART`, the 27 approved grids (20 props, 7 tiles), PAL keys only, byte for byte as Design's `weir_hut_art.json`. The lamp shelf and the trough loop two frames; reduced motion holds frame 0.
  - `game.js`: `cellImg` draws a two-frame skin tile on the water clock (frame 0 under reduced motion); `wildBaseRange` rolls `hw_falls` and `hw_cut` at Wickerglen's Lv 13-16 until `story.cobble_end` (D1594 a); `checkTriggers` skips a trigger whose scene is not built, so the first-step trigger `chi_hut_first` -> `chi_meet` (D1598) is a stub that does nothing until the meeting build adds that scene (it was the only such trigger); `hw_weir_hut_in` joins `REGION_MAP.interiors` under `hw_cut`.
  - `battle_bg.js`: `hw_weir_hut_in` gets a battle scene (its first two props) so every room keeps one; the three FERNBROOK_HOMES rooms, which had none on main (`tests/battle_backdrops.spec.js` failed there), get theirs too.
  - `data/reputation.json`: a neutral cast entry for Vincent Chi (the boot's cast check needs one for every NPC name). His look `chi` is not built here, so he draws as the default villager until the meeting build adds it.
  - The cot is a line, not a rest (D1596). `story.chi_jars` (0-6, placeholder name) drives the six jars, one prop per sill tile (D1593). `story.chi_met` / `story.chi_done` are read, never written.
  - On main `story.warden_met` is not set by anything yet (the crackdown branch, #288, sets it), so in normal play the hut is reachable only after Hall 2 (`story.cobble_end`), or under `?debug=1`.

## 2026-11-03 14:18 ET: Build: Governor Merriden opens in Act 2 in the city preview (D1426, D1439, HIGH_CHARITY_LAYOUT v0.4 §5.3)
- What changed and why:
  - D1426 and D1439 (red, SETTLED by Vincent, Tue Nov 3, 2026): the holder meets the Governor in Act 2, before the President. In `tools/build_hc_city.py` the Governor's House door (`hc_gov`) now needs `!story.side=glimmer & story.act>=2` (was `>=5`), still "By appointment." otherwise; Governor Merriden stands at his desk from `story.act>=2`; his PLACEHOLDER line says "Act 2 lines are Story's". The ORDERS DOWN chute (HB12) is on floor 1, so it opens with the door.
  - The Governor is a civil post, not a Keeper: his cast faction is `none` (the cast only knows `keepers`, `glimmer` and `none`; `none` is what civilians use), so he no longer needs a D1374 rank flag.
  - The President's rooms (`hc_gov_3`) keep their gate: Keepers path, Act 5, after meeting the Governor. The Cabinet Room (`hc_gov_2`, floor 2) is not built yet (phase 2); the tool notes it follows the Act 2 gate when it lands.
  - `story.act`: `cond()` reads it through `flagOf`. When this was built nothing wrote it; since #297 (merged first) it is derived from flags (`PPStanding.actOf`), so the Keepers path reaches Act 2 in play after the Episode 1 card. Debug places seed it (honoured under `?debug=1`).

- What changed and why: on main (af1ba3f), `tests/phone_contacts.spec.js` failed at 320 and 390 with an extra "Mom", and `tests/tap_interact.spec.js` failed at 320 with talk +12, trigger +2, warp +4. The tap audit counts now include the objects #258 added: hm_home_hall and hm_home_room, Mom and Maze downstairs, the pegs, the cushion and two Hearthmoor lines (talk 741, warp 100, trigger 40). The audit also pins the page clock to an ET midday, so a time-gated object can never make it flaky. No game code or data change, no save change.

## 2026-11-02 16:07 ET: Faction pick step 1: the live-save OR gate (no save change)

- What changed and why: FACTION_PICK_TIMING v0.2.4 §7 step 1 (design ffada0b), built on its own before steps 2-5. Vincent approved the seven reds (D1141-D1143, D1149-D1152) on Nov 2 at 2:35 PM ET. The four condition readers now take `|` (or): `cond()` and the boot validator's condition check (now `condKeyOk`, shared with `glimmerDevCheck`) in `game.js`, `evalCond` in `quests.js` (which also gets `&`), and `plainStoryFlag` (the debug checkpoints). Every reader splits `|` first, then `&`, then `!`, with no brackets, so `a | b & c` reads as a, or (b and c). `plainStoryFlag` treats a `|` or `&` string as more than one flag and writes nothing for it, so a checkpoint never writes a literal "a|b" key. Today's gates move to their §7.1 OR forms: the Route 2 warp, open sign, rubble and closed sign; Rook's teaser (`rook_fb`, `fb_teaser`); the Hall door `need`; the two Hall slips on the town board; the `heights` skipIf; and the STANDING tab. The maps_slice.json and act1_route2.json objects are restated by id in fernbrook_rooms.json's slicePatch `replaceObjs`, with only `when` changed; both verbatim files are untouched. Nothing sets the new flags yet (`story.crates_seen`, `story.pitch_k`, `story.pitch_g`), so every gate reads the same on every save. Two read-only debug hooks (`PixelPets.condRead`, `PixelPets.gateRead`, plus `PixelPets.standingTab`) return null without `?debug=1`.
- Files touched: `game.js`, `quests.js`, `data/fernbrook_rooms.json`, `data/town_board.json`, `data/mapdata.js` (regenerated), `tools/GAMEJS_MAP.txt` (regenerated), `tests/faction_or_gate.spec.js` (new), `tests/fixtures/or_gate_kinds.js` (new), `tests/fixtures/or_gate_before.json` (new), `tests/README.md`, `index.html`, `version.json`, `CHANGELOG.md`.
- Commit(s) + build id: this branch, build 20261102-1607.
- Tests run and result (widths, checks): `tests/faction_or_gate.spec.js` at 320, 375 and 390: T-ORDER on all four readers, the gate matrix on 15 save fixtures (the seven §7.4 kinds plus eight cuts of them) against the matrix recorded on origin/main 8bdb485 (zero diffs), the §7.3 save proof on the seven kinds on a clean build (byte-identical after load, v stays 2), the overlay check, and reduced motion. A mutation run (with `&` split before `|` in `cond()` and `evalCond`) fails T-ORDER. Full suite result line in the PR.
- Decisions made (colour + reasoning): The reds and yellows are Design's and Vincent's (FACTION_PICK_TIMING §4). Green, inside the spec: `plainStoryFlag` returns no flag for a `|` or `&` string (the spec only rules out a literal "a|b" key); the boot validator trims each part, as `cond()` does. The Heights door `need` OR waits for step 3, where §7 puts it with the `ESCORT_STAIRS` key. No new D ids.
- Anything left unfinished: Steps 2-5. The copy changes in §2.1 and §7.1 (June's Hall door deny line, the latched slip, the Heights deny lines) and the STANDING tab's pre-pick PLEDGED and `sides` tip changes come in later briefs. `data/town_board_copy.json` (not loaded by the game) still shows the old slip `when`. D1144, D1153 and D1156 are yellows that settle at 5:44 PM EST today, so the PR stays a draft until then.

## 2026-10-31 19:44 ET: NEWS app and a fuller Ribbit feed, written from the lore (D1012-D1016, build 20261031-1944)

- What changed and why: the owner asked for a fully fleshed-out news feed with constant updates about the lore, plus a richer social app, with lore entries in our files so skills can keep it stocked; after the first cut they asked for livelier, more dynamic posts and a better layout, and picked the tabbed paper from three rendered mockups. The phone gets a tenth app, NEWS: a Block News paper with a ruled masthead and an edition bar (MORNING, EVENING, NIGHT), four single-tap tabs and more colour. FRONT: a BREAKING bar when the lead changed since your last read, a wire line you step with one tap, the lead with big Walkling art (headline and first line; "Read the full story" opens the rest and its Ribbit replies), and three "also today" rows. READ: section accordions in their colours, stories open on a tap. TOWN: three Voices from town, notices and classifieds (Glimmerwell ads on the cracked handset only) and a one-tap daily poll with seeded results. YOU: an "about you" piece (your name, partner, steps, Seals, town), stat tiles, and a Walkling of the day drawn only from forms you have seen. Stories react to story progress, the real day, the live sky and the game clock. Asked for one or two more ideas and more to read, the owner picked the Scrapbook: every front page you read is clipped once into YOU and pays 3 Acorns (D1016; at most 66 over Episode 1); leads you passed without opening the paper come in as unpaid back issues; torn-edged clippings in story order open on a tap and never show an unreached lead. The pool: 186 stories, 124 voices, 76 notices, 24 polls, 30 "about you" pieces, plus 135 Ribbit posts and 12 tags; Ribbit covers Mahopac, High Charity, Route 3 and Brinewick and shows three desk posts a day. Every item cites ids from the design repo's lore index and passes its checker (BUILT facts or CANON behind reveal flags; nothing PLAN or OPEN; chips only on the Glimmer side); flavour is allowed under D1015 (no new names, places, events or numbers). The copy is placeholder until Design signs it (D1013). Red D1012 settled by the owner ("Merge it, I approve", Oct 31); the preview screenshots lived on the PR and are not committed.
- Files touched: `news.js` (new), `data/news.json` and `data/ribbit_posts.json` (new), `data/mapdata.js`, `tools/gen_mapdata.py`, `ribbit.js`, `game.js` (NEWS block), `index.html`, `style.css`, `save.js` (`newsSeen`, `newsTab`, `newsPoll`, `newsClips`), `sw.js`, `tests/news.spec.js` (new), `tests/phone_contacts.spec.js`, `tests/README.md`, `.cursor/skills/walklings-lore-feed/` and `walklings-lore-index/` (new), `.cursor/skills/README.md`, `AGENTS.md` (skills index), `OPEN_DECISIONS.md`, `BACKLOG.md`, `CHANGELOG.md`, `tools/GAMEJS_MAP.txt`, `version.json`, `archive/DECISIONS_SETTLED.md`.

## 2026-10-31 11:21 ET: First-area wild balance: wilds follow the lead, a full-HP wild survives one hit (build 20261031-1342)

- What changed and why: In Route 1 a starter one-shot most wilds, so there was nothing to wear down and befriend. Research (in-game formulas): walking and care XP count x2 toward level, so a starter reaches Lv 7-8 on day 1 and Lv 10-12 by day 3 while Route 1 wilds stay Lv 2-6; under the default meadow tag 70% of Route 1 spawns are BLOOM-type, which Ember hits for x2.5. A Lv 8 Ember one-shot 73% of Route 1 wilds, a Lv 12 Ember 84% of Fernbrook's. Two changes (options B + C, chosen by the user in this session): (B) WILD_FOLLOW: on Route 1 and Fernbrook only, a wild rolls between lead - 2 and the lead's level, held under a cap (Route 1 Lv 9, Fernbrook Lv 13) and never below its table row; the Hearthmoor guide wild stays Lv 3; other maps unchanged. (C) WILD_GUARD: in a wild battle that is not a boss, a wild at full HP keeps 1 HP from one hit by the holder unless the holder is 10+ levels above it (the big-gap rule). The guard clamps `r.dmg`, so the damage number matches the HP lost. Battles with other holders, bosses (D930 owns those) and foe hits on the holder (D928) are unchanged. Modelled result: Ember on Route 1 at Lv 7-12 goes from 52-92% one-shots to 0%, and the befriend bar after the first hit stays about 0.24-0.28.
- Files touched: `game.js` (wildBaseRange, WILD_FOLLOW, wildLvRange, wildLv, the guard in doMove, read-only `PixelPets.wildLvRange` / `PixelPets.wildGuard`), `stats.js` (`STAT_RULES.WILD_GUARD`, `wildGuardDmg`), `tests/wild_level_follow.spec.js` (new), `tests/README.md`, `CHANGELOG.md`, `tools/GAMEJS_MAP.txt`, `index.html`, `version.json`.

## 2026-10-31 11:20 ET: NPC_LIFE: people walk short routes, look around, and react when you talk (build 20261031-1339)

- What changed and why: Vincent asked that every NPC who talks to the holder shows clear reactions to what the holder says, is animated a bit, and has a walking path like Warden Ilse and Rook have in their scenes. Talking: the npc hops when spoken to and bobs 1 px while its line types out; a line raises a balloon that fits its tone (`?` ask, `!` bang, laughs, thanks and leading dots); every answer the holder picks gets a reaction from whoever asked (yes, thanks, help or a purchase: heart + bounce; no, not now, later: dots + head shake; battle or match: the match balloon; anything else: "!" + hop). The reaction outlives the scene, so a "Not now" that closes the talk still shows. Ambient chatter barks add a hop. Walking: about 60 of the 105 npcs present on a fresh save, plus the clock-scheduled people, now walk a 1-2 tile back-and-forth route from their home tile, with a pause of 2-5 s at each end, and glance aside now and then when standing; they wait and face the holder within 2 tiles and stay put while tap-to-talk walks up. Routes are worked out once per map visit, one npc per 100 ms tick, and never use a door, doorstep, warp, any object's tile, a battling holder's sight line, another walker's tiles or a one-wide gap, so no walker opens or closes a way through. Schedules (Vincent: "rework schedules"): a clock-scheduled person's home is the tile of their current row, so Pip, Wren, Lizzy, Mr. Gristle, Ossabell and the store keepers walk around wherever the clock puts them; when the clock moves them they stand on the new row's tile and work out a new walk there, and the row a person is seen on still holds while they are on screen. Quest arrows that point at a person's home tile follow them while they walk. Shop counters, meal counters, the wardrobe, Lantern guides, panels, booths, the board, match walk-ups (Rook at the gate), catch npcs (Officer Ness), pets, time echoes and scene cast stand still but still react and glance. Officer Bren's authored patrol now glides between tiles instead of jumping. Reduced motion: nobody walks or glances and there is no hop, bob or shake; the balloons still show. Runtime only: nothing is saved, and each map visit starts everyone at home.

## 2026-10-28 12:40 ET: Two racy specs made steady: the follower-arrow check and the move-FX turn timer (tests only, no build)

- What changed and why: found by the full suite on the checks branch (1967 passed, 5 failed, 2 flaky; the failures were the four co-op specs, which need the sandbox CA in Chromium, and `moves_v2` 7). `partner_care` "a quest arrow on the follower tile ... stays put" waited only for `world.x/y`, which switch when a step starts, so about one run in four read the arrow while the keeper (and the camera) were still mid-step and the arrow sat 1-2 px off. It now waits for the walk to finish. `moves_v2` 7 timed a turn by the wall clock, which also counted Playwright's own click waits (about 260 ms on FIGHT; the turn itself is about 280 ms on FULL), and with OFF the turn could leave and re-enter CHOOSE between two polls, so the wait hung. It now times the turn in the page, from the move tap to the next CHOOSE of a later turn (`battle.turn`), against the same 700 / 400 / 250 ms budgets.

## 2026-10-28 08:30 ET: Co-op test harness races and the arbiter_units line race (tests only, no build)

- What changed and why: The co-op specs (`art_pass_coop`, `coop_r2`, `play_together`, `moves_v2`) timed out at 390 in every full run here. Root cause one is this sandbox, not the repo: with the proxy unset, Chromium reaches www.gstatic.com through an egress gateway whose CA is in the OpenSSL store but not in Chromium's NSS store, so the pinned Firebase SDK never loads (`net::ERR_CERT_AUTHORITY_INVALID`). With that CA trusted in a scratch NSS store, real races showed and are fixed: the emulator beforeAll hooks in `art_pass_coop` and `moves_v2` get the 600 s timeout the other co-op specs use; `tests/emu_shared.js` waits for an emulator this run already started (instead of failing "previous emulator did not exit"), allows 120 s for a cold start, and warms the local Firestore emulator (one owner read, one rules-checked read) before any page loads; `coop_r2` ignores `rev` and `updatedAt` in the save comparison (cloud.js stamps them on its own timer and its own change hash skips them); `play_together` samples the remote glide every frame until the copy arrives, and "14 area and 15 interior" gets back its 50 s cap from before f51b4a4; `moves_v2` 13 turns tips off so the party tip does not cover MOVES. `arbiter_units:108` read a line that `textMs = 1` replaced within a millisecond; the test now holds that one line, asserts it, then asserts the prompt returns. No assertion loosened; nothing skipped; no game code.
- Files touched: `tests/emu_shared.js`, `tests/art_pass_coop.spec.js`, `tests/coop_r2.spec.js`, `tests/play_together.spec.js`, `tests/moves_v2.spec.js`, `tests/arbiter_units.spec.js`, `CHANGELOG.md`.
- Commit(s) + build id: this branch; tests only, so no build stamp.
- Tests run and result (widths, checks): `arbiter_units` at 320/375/390: 33 passed (and 99 of 99 with `--repeat-each=3`). Co-op specs at 390 with `--repeat-each=3` and the scratch CA trust: all five originally failing tests 3 of 3. Still failing here: `coop_r2:506` launches WebKit, which this container does not have; `moves_v2` 7 and 20 time wall-clock in the test process and miss under heavy load.
- Decisions made (colour + reasoning): green. No ids.
- Anything left unfinished: the sandbox CA (an environment setting, not the repo); `moves_v2` 7 and 20 should measure inside the page as test 21 does.

## 2026-10-28 06:25 ET: Play together and battery fixes from the review (build 20261028-0625)

- What changed and why: the medium and low items from the Oct 28 memory and crash review, cleared by Vincent ("go ahead"). Play together (`net.js`): position and heartbeat writes wait for the one in flight, so a slow or dead signal no longer queues a write every 100 ms and bursts into the rate limit; the emote wheel is rebuilt only when its cooldown starts or ends (it was rebuilt 10 times a second and swallowed taps); coming back from the back/forward cache drops the stale room node and rejoins (other holders could no longer see you); rejected writes in away/back, pagehide, inbox drop, `syncLook` and `repair` are caught (they were console errors); a held invite is kept once per sender and invite. Game (`game.js`): the battle/pet backdrop cache keeps the newest 8 canvases (it grew with every iPhone resize); the 120 ms UI tick and the 500 ms build-bar pump skip work while the page is hidden; the open guard forgets held fingers on hide (iOS can drop touchend under a system gesture).

## 2026-10-28 06:23 ET: The game keeps running after a frame error; the offline cache stops growing; a failed save is told (build 20261028-0623)

- What changed and why: three fixes from the Oct 28 memory and crash review, cleared by Vincent. (1) The main loop scheduled its next frame on its last line, so one error in any frame froze the map for the rest of the session while saves and timers ran on. The next frame is now always scheduled; the error still reaches the console and `pp_errors`, and after 10 failing frames in a row the loop retries once a second. (2) The service worker stored every `?v=<build>` copy of each script under its own key in a cache that never changes name, so each build a player loaded added about 2.5 MB that was never removed. Files are now stored under their plain path, and the new worker's activate step deletes the old query-keyed copies. `clock.js`, `mv_fx.js` and `net.js` join the precache list (an offline first launch lost the clock, move effects and Play together). (3) `save()` ignored a failed write (storage full or blocked, or another tab took the save), so progress could stop saving silently. The player now gets one toast per page load: "Couldn't save. Close other Walklings tabs, or free some space, then reload."
- Files touched: `game.js` (`frame`/`frameBody`, `save`, debug `breakFrames`/`frameCount`), `sw.js`, `tests/loop_save_sw.spec.js` (new), `tests/README.md`, `index.html`, `version.json`, `tools/GAMEJS_MAP.txt`, `CHANGELOG.md`.
- Commit(s) + build id: `fix/loop-guard-sw-cache-save` from main `a7db11b`. Build `20261028-0623`.
- Tests run and result (widths, checks): `tests/loop_save_sw.spec.js` 5 passed at 320/375/390 (the frame row 15/15 on repeat); with main's `sw.js` and `save()` the worker and save rows fail. Boot, reload, crash, deploy and save suites (11 files): 135 passed, 1 failed once: `crash_diag` 320 "renderer crash" compares the service-worker-controlled flag across a reload and passed 27/27 on repeat. `debug_mode` passes. Scans all PASS.
- Decisions made (colour + reasoning): green. Bug fixes; no player-facing change except the one save-failure toast. Debug hooks stay inert without ?debug=1.
- Anything left unfinished: the medium and low review items (net.js write pile-up, emote wheel repaint, bfcache rejoin, unhandled rejections, uncapped bdCache, background timers, stuck pointers).

## 2026-10-27 16:52 ET: `npm run test:agent` works on Windows (no build stamp change)

- What changed and why: On Windows the runner could not start its helpers. `npx` and `node_modules/.bin/firebase` are `.cmd` shims, which Node's `spawn` cannot run without a shell, so the Playwright stage and the rules step both failed; and the emulator cleanup used process groups and `/proc`, which Windows does not have, so every run left a `java.exe` emulator behind. On Windows only, Playwright and the firebase CLI are now started as `node <their own JS file>` (the file the shim runs), with no shell, and `tools/emu_ports.js` ends the CLI's whole process tree with `taskkill /T /F` (it only touches a pid that is still a `node.exe`, and also finds a database-emulator java left on our port). `removeDir` retries on Windows because the folder stays locked for a moment after the kill. Every new path is behind `process.platform === 'win32'`; the other systems start the same commands as before. No game or player-facing file changed.
- Files touched: `tools/emu_ports.js` (`firebaseLaunch`, Windows kill and retry), `tools/pp_test.js`, `tools/rtdb_rules_test.js`, `tests/emu_shared.js`, `tests/README.md`, `CHANGELOG.md`.
- Commit(s) + build id: `claude/windows-test-runner`. No deployed file changed, so this PR adds no stamp; the build is whatever `main` has (`20261028-0858` when `main` was merged in) and `bump_build.py --check` passes inside `test:agent`.
- Tests run and result (widths, checks): on Windows 11, Node 24, JDK 21: `npm run test:agent -- boot_to_map.spec.js --project=390` four times (two with the output discarded, so only their exit code was read) → `PP_TEST RESULT: PASS`, 4 passed, `RTDB_RULES RESULT: PASS`, no `java.exe` left afterwards. `coop_r2.spec.js` at 390 (uses the shared emulator in `tests/emu_shared.js`) → 9 passed, 1 skipped, 1 failed: `coop_r2.spec.js:501` opens WebKit, which is not installed on that machine (not caused by this change). Not run on Linux or macOS; their code paths are unchanged by construction.
- Decisions made (colour + reasoning): green; test tooling only, no player-visible or save change.
- Anything left unfinished: Windows still needs a `python3` command on `PATH` (`tools/pp_test.js`, `tools/scans/gamejs_map.js`, `tools/scans/selftest.js`, the Playwright `webServer` and a few specs call `python3`; python.org's installer only creates `python.exe`). The other `.cmd`/`python3` call sites were left alone. WebKit is not installed there, so `coop_r2.spec.js:501` needs `npx playwright install webkit` first.

## 2026-10-27 11:04 ET: Modern by default, and arcade looks like a controller (build 20261027-1104)

- What changed and why: Vincent asked (Oct 27, 2026) for three things. Modern controls are now the default: a new game and any save with no controls choice load as modern, a save that picked arcade keeps arcade, and the house prompt offers MODERN first (D833). Arcade held sideways keeps the D-pad but takes modern's layout and icons: the pad moves to a left grip and TALK becomes a round A button on a right grip, both outside the game window, with the column centred and capped at 480 (D834). Arcade upright is skinned like a handheld: the map sits in a lighter screen bezel above a controller body with a one-piece dark cross and round PHONE, BAG and MAP face buttons; paint only, so no rect changes (D835). `ctlPick()` is the saved choice; `ctlMode()` stays the layout. `LAND_MOVES` re-parents `#dpad` and `#talkBtn` into `#gripPad` and `#gripTalk`, so each keeps its one handler. Turning the phone re-lays at once and again 250 ms later, lets go of held input, and saves nothing. Because a new game now starts in modern, the dock gear had to work in the opening scene: the dock's scene rule hid it with opacity (which a child cannot undo) and blocked its taps, so a player who pressed Start over could not open Settings to bring back a copy. The dock now hides with visibility and the gear stays tappable unless `gear-locked` (a scene once you have a partner) applies.

## 2026-10-25 16:10 ET: The crash note waits for boot scenes, and the 320 arcade card is a short row (build 20261025-1606)

- What changed and why: PR #188 was held because the crash note checked for a scene only once, inside `boot()`, before the opening scene started. A crash with no save played "Morning! Sunlight pokes through the shutters." with the note already up, and it stayed up. The note now stays hidden while a scene, a tutorial, a battle, or an overlay is open, including one that starts after boot. The first show waits 500 ms so the opening scene (60 ms) and a resumed tour (400 ms) have started. A watcher on `hidden` and `class`, plus a 200 ms poll, hides the note as soon as one of those opens and shows it again when they close. It is marked seen only after it has stayed on screen for 300 ms, so a flash before a scene does not count. OK dismisses it for good. If a panel opens while the note is up, the note steps aside and comes back, unless OK was already tapped. The copy is unchanged: `The game closed unexpectedly. Tap Copy crash info in Settings.` At 320 arcade the map is about 150 px tall and the old card was 116 px, so it covered most of the map. At that width in arcade the note is one row, 52 px tall, with the copy on two lines and OK at 44 by 44. Wider arcade screens and modern controls keep the stacked card. A planned `boot` reload still counts the crash and does not show the note. `sw.js` is unchanged. Save stays `pixelpets.save.v2` with `v` 2 and gains no field.

## 2026-10-25 15:30 ET: Crash log, heartbeat fields, and a one-time note after a crash (build 20261025-1353)

- What changed and why: Supervisor green brief (Oct 25, 2026), plus a one-time crash note the project owner approved and the Supervisor relayed at 1:12 PM ET. A crash is still a boot that finds `pp_alive` and no `pp_reload`. Those rows used to live only in `pp_reload_last`, which keeps five. A new key, `pp_crash_log`, is never trimmed. Per build it stores the count, the first time, the last time, and how many notes were shown. It also keeps the last crashed page's `pp_diag`. Copy crash info uses that stash (`diagFrom: crash`) so the notes are the crashed page, not the boot that copied them. If there is no stash it still reads the current `pp_diag` and labels it `this-boot`. The button shows when any of the last five reloads is a crash, or this build's count is above zero, or the page is `?debug=1`. Settings adds `Crashes on this build: N` when N is above zero, and hides the line when it is zero so a clean Settings panel does not move. Each heartbeat also stores the view size, orientation, device pixel ratio, whether a service worker is controlling the page, the registration state, and milliseconds since the last tap. The registration lookup is cached and does not block the beat. `fitAll` now skips resetting the map canvas width and height when the size is already right, the same guard `fitBattle` already had, so a resize that does not change the size does not wipe the bitmap. The first boot after a crash shows one card the holder can dismiss: `The game closed unexpectedly. Tap Copy crash info in Settings.` It is marked seen in `pp_crash_log` when it is shown, not in the save. A planned reload, a background close, or an unknown reload does not show it. The card waits while a tutorial, a scene, a battle, or an overlay is up. The starting line said "Your progress was saved." That sentence is not in the card. `save()` runs every 5 seconds after boot, and on hide or `pagehide` only while a battle is open. A renderer crash runs neither, and a battle is not restored on the next boot, so the last few seconds are not guaranteed. `sw.js` is unchanged. Save stays `pixelpets.save.v2` with `v` 2 and gains no field.
- Files touched: `game.js`, `style.css`, `index.html`, `version.json`, `tools/GAMEJS_MAP.txt`, `tests/crash_diag.spec.js`, `tests/crash_copy.spec.js`, `tests/README.md`, `README.md`, `CHANGELOG.md`.

Full suite (`PP_PORT=8836`): `PP_TEST RESULT: PASS exit=0 passed=1650 failed=0 flaky=0 skipped=207 time=3365s`. RTDB_RULES RESULT: PASS. ## 2026-10-25 08:45 ET: Damage numbers no longer vanish before their first frame (build 20261025-0736)

- What changed and why: Supervisor green call (Oct 25, 2026, no new D id), follow-up to the race reported in the 07:10 entry. `mvNumDraw` in `mv_fx.js` turned a damage number off when `age = t - s.t0` was below 0. `t0` is `performance.now()` from the queue task that takes the number, but the draw uses the rAF frame stamp, which can be a little earlier. When that happened the number was dropped before it was ever drawn, so players sometimes saw no damage number on a hit. Line 216 is now `if (age < 0) continue; if (age >= dur) { s.on = 0; continue; }`: a frame with a negative age skips the number and the next frame draws it. Numbers still end at the same time (440 ms, calm 600 ms). `moves_v2` test 20 is now a regression test and runs at 320, 375 and 390 (the `@w1` tag is gone). A debug hook in the test (`hookNumbers`) wraps `mvNumTake` and `mvNumDraw` and logs each number taken and whether a frame drew it. The meadow hit uses the normal timing. On the snow hit the number starts 50 ms after the take, so the first frames always see a negative age. The test checks that each battle takes 1 number, that none is dropped before its first draw, that every number is drawn, and (snow) that a frame did come before the number's start. The dark outline checks stay as they were. Save stays `pixelpets.save.v2` with `v` 2 and gains no field.

## 2026-10-25 07:10 ET: moves_v2 test 21 pins the battle roll and reads the white frames on the game's clock (tests only, no new build)

- What changed and why: `moves_v2` test 21 ("a super-effective white frame is one run of about 80 ms") failed about 1 run in 5 at 390 on main and on branches. Root cause: the test never pinned the battle roll, although the comments in tests 16, 18 and 19 and the 05:30 entry below say it did. `foxfire_flick` is 95% accurate, so a miss (roll 0.95 or more) drew no white frame and `runs` came back empty. A lucky hit (1 in 16) knocked the 32 HP Lv4 foe out, so the battle went to END, never got back to CHOOSE, and the wait timed out after 30 s. Two battles per test at about 11% each is about 21% a run, which matches 3 of 15 and 4 of 15. The test now sets `b.rng = () => 0.5` like tests 16, 18, 19 and 20. The wall-clock check (`view.white` read from the test every 5 ms) is gone too. A getter/setter on `PixelPets.battle.view`, set up inside the test, now logs every frame the battle canvas draws with the game's own draw time (`view.t`). The test checks that at least one white frame was drawn, that the white frames come one after another with no gap, that each one falls inside the recorded `[t0, until)` window, and that every frame drawn inside the window is white. The existing checks stay: exactly 1 white run, 70 to 90 ms long, for both FULL and OFF. Lines last 200 ms in this test instead of 0 (players get 600 ms or more, or a tap). With 0, effects OFF has no hit-stop, so the rest of the turn ran as back-to-back 0 ms timers and under load the canvas drew no frame for more than 380 ms after the hit, missing the whole 80 ms window. The screenshot `super-hit-390.png` is taken once a white frame has been logged. No game file changed. Save stays `pixelpets.save.v2` with `v` 2 and gains no field.
- Files touched: `tests/moves_v2.spec.js`, `CHANGELOG.md`.
- Commit(s) + build id: `b1b763d` (pin the roll, log drawn frames), `04c036c` (200 ms lines), plus this entry, on `cursor/moves-v2-t21-stable` from main `68c273f`. Tests only, so not stamped. Live build stays `20261025-0511`.
- Tests run and result (widths, checks): Before the fix, test 21 at 390 with `--repeat-each=15 --retries=0`: `PP_TEST RESULT: FAIL exit=1 passed=13 failed=2` (one empty `runs`, one 30 s timeout waiting for CHOOSE). A scratch probe with scripted rolls showed it: roll 0.97 gave `whites=[]`, a lucky roll of 0.01 left the foe at 0/32 with the battle in END, and 0.5 gave `whites=[80]` with the battle back at CHOOSE. After the fix, test 21 at 390 with `--repeat-each=30 --retries=0`: `PP_TEST RESULT: PASS exit=0 passed=30 failed=0 flaky=0 skipped=0 time=74s`. The whole `moves_v2` spec at 320, 375 and 390 with `--repeat-each=5 --retries=0`: `PP_TEST RESULT: PASS exit=0 passed=145 failed=0 flaky=0 skipped=180 time=170s`. `aura_fx`, `battle_no_reload` and `group_battle` at 320, 375 and 390: `PP_TEST RESULT: PASS exit=0 passed=44 failed=0 flaky=0 skipped=4 time=74s`. `p0_trainer_win`: `PP_TEST RESULT: PASS exit=0 passed=1 failed=0`. `python3 tools/bump_build.py --check` exits 0. `node tools/scans/run.js` prints SCAN PASS 5 times. `node tools/scans/selftest.js` prints SCAN SELFTEST PASS.
- Decisions made (colour + reasoning): No new D or Q id. Green: a test-only change that checks at least as much as before.
- Anything left unfinished: `moves_v2` test 20 at 320 ("damage digits keep a dark outline", timed out at line 724 in 1 of 10 runs alone) is a game race, not a test problem, so it is reported here and not fixed. `mvNumDraw` in `mv_fx.js` turns a damage number off when `age = t - s.t0` is below 0. `t0` is `performance.now()` from the queue task, but the draw uses the rAF frame timestamp, which can be a little earlier, so the number is dropped before it is ever drawn. A scratch probe of 40 pinned hits at 320 lost the number 4 times, and each of those 4 had a first-draw age of -0.1 to -0.0x ms. None of the 36 hits where the number was drawn had a negative age. Players would sometimes see no damage number. A possible fix (for the engineer, needs clearing): clamp `age` to 0, or drop the number only when `age >= dur`.

## 2026-10-25 05:30 ET: Map zoom: D787 overlap pads and the D729 arcade chip gated off until MODERN_HUD_CLEAR P1, zoom steps that keep 7 x 8.5 tiles, the Map zoom row lines up (build 20261025-0511)

The Map zoom label lines up with the other Settings rows (left edge 43 px, was 37), and the 44 px thumb stays at least 12 px from the label and the value at every step at 320, 375 and 390. ## 2026-10-22 02:17 ET: List portraits are reused, and a silent page death records why (build 20261022-0217)

- What changed and why: People and Contacts were building a new canvas for every row on every open, and the full-frame cache held eight canvases per look with a small cap. A warm People open now draws nothing new: one front frame per look, and the row canvases are pooled across People and Contacts. Standing emblems are pooled the same way. The full-frame cache stays, with a larger least-recently-used cap as a backstop. A jingle disconnects its shared gain when it ends. The reload line can name the open screen. A battle still says in battle, and the heartbeat also stores the battle phase (hit while a move resolves, end on the end panel). The same beat stores the live canvas count, the portrait-cache size, and how many canvases were made since the previous beat, without walking the document. Those counts are copied onto a crash or background-kill entry. A hidden page writes `pp_hidden`. If the next boot still finds `pp_alive` and that hidden mark, the line says background kill instead of crash?. The stored entry also keeps the navigation type, whether the page is a home-screen app, whether a service worker controlled the page, the gap since the last beat, the hidden mark and its age, and `wasDiscarded` when the browser provides it. Settings stays one short line. The last five reloads, with every field, and the last five script errors show only on a `?debug=1` page. All of that lives in plain localStorage. The save stays `pixelpets.save.v2` with `v` 2. No new save field.

## 2026-10-19 21:43 ET: P0 hotfix: nothing reloads the page under a battle, plus a reload breadcrumb (build 20261019-2143)

- What changed and why: Vincent (iPhone Safari, 21:27 ET): "During battles, I've had safari refresh three times, making trainer battles impossible to progress through." A battle is never saved mid-fight, so any reload restarts a trainer. Two code paths could reload under a battle. (1) Cloud adopt (PR #99, live since 17:53 ET): when the first cloud compare timed out, `cloud.js` retried it every 10 s with no battle check, and a quiet adopt called `location.reload()` straight away (`game.js` startCloud `adopt`). Now `sync()` skips the compare while `busy()` (any overlay or `battle.on`), and `adopt` itself refuses under an overlay or battle. The next tick after the battle compares, so local changes from the battle become the existing "which game to keep" prompt and are never thrown away. (2) The "New build ready" bar was polled every 500 ms, so it stayed over the first ≤500 ms of a battle (over the foe card) and a tap reloaded. `openBattle` now hides it at once, `pumpBuildBar` also checks `battle.on`, and `#buildGo` ignores taps under an overlay or battle. Diagnostics: every reload or replace path writes localStorage `pp_reload` {reason, at, battleOn, build} first (cloud, build, boot, startover, copy, bfcache, newer); pagehide/beforeunload with none writes 'unknown'. `pp_alive` is set while visible and cleared on hide, so a boot with `pp_alive` but no `pp_reload` logs 'crash'. Boot moves the entry to `pp_reload_last` (last 5) and Settings shows "Last reload: cloud, 9:22 PM, in battle" under the build line. Measured: 12 battles in a row at 390 show flat heap (5.5 -> 6.1 MB), DOM, canvases, intervals and rAF, so a memory kill is unlikely. Save `v` stays 2 (the new keys are outside the save).
- Files touched: `game.js`, `cloud.js`, `index.html`, `tests/battle_no_reload.spec.js` (new), `tests/README.md`, `CHANGELOG.md`, `version.json`.
- Commit(s) + build id: branch `hotfix/battle-no-reload`, build 20261019-2143.
- Tests run and result (widths, checks): `node tools/pp_test.js tests/battle_no_reload.spec.js tests/p0_trainer_win.spec.js tests/group_battle.spec.js tests/cloud_off.spec.js tests/ios_reload.spec.js tests/save_copy.spec.js tests/smoke.spec.js` PP_TEST RESULT: PASS exit=0 passed=85 failed=0 flaky=0 skipped=2 time=138s (widths 320, 375, 390; the 2 skips are the cloud timing check at 320/375, run at 390 only). The cloud check fails on the unguarded code (a compare ran under the battle). `python3 tools/bump_build.py --check` exit 0. Console errors: none.
- Decisions made (colour + reasoning): green, P0 hotfix approved by the Supervisor. No new id.
- Anything left unfinished: no mid-battle checkpoint (a reload still restarts the fight; now nothing in the game triggers one under a battle).
